add_namespace = fcf_politics_events
country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.0
	title = {
		text = fcf_politics_events.0.title.a
		trigger = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } }
	}
	title = {
		text = fcf_politics_events.0.title.b
		trigger = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_politics_events.0.desc.a
		trigger = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = fcf_politics_events.0.desc.b
		trigger = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } } }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.0.a
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434 }
				check_variable = { agreement_vote_count > 433 }
			}
			else = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = voting_for_change_csp }
			meta_effect = {
				text = { CSP_appoint_[CSPVAR]_effect = yes }
				CSPVAR = "[?target_CSP_member_id]"
			}
		}
		else = {
			meta_effect = {
				text = { bill_[BILLVAR]_pass_effect = yes }
				BILLVAR = "[?bill_var]"
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.0.b
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434 }
				check_variable = { agreement_vote_count < 434 }
			}
			else = { NOT = { check_expr = { value = 650 subtract = abstention_vote_count divide = 2 less_than = agreement_vote_count } } }
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	after = {
		set_variable = { bill_var = 0 }
		clr_country_flag = voting_for_bill
		clr_country_flag = voting_for_change_csp
		set_variable = { agreement_vote_count = 0 }
		set_variable = { abstention_vote_count = 0 }
		set_variable = { eligible_vote_count = 650 }
		clr_country_flag = bill_pass_need_434

		clr_country_flag = PSF_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = POU_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = PCOP_abstain_vote
		clr_country_flag = PAPF_abstain_vote

		politics_CGF_seats_refresh = yes
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.1

	desc = fcf_politics_events.1.desc
	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.1.a
		hidden_effect = { FCF_economy_supply_chain_modifier_update = yes }
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.1.b
		hidden_effect = {
			set_cosmetic_tag = FRA_rouge
			FCF_economy_supply_chain_modifier_update = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.2
	title = fcf_politics_events.2.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.2.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = -2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = -4 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
			politics_CGF_seat_change = yes

			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_CNS = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_PSF = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_POU = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_CGT = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_PCOP = 2 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_UA = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_URSRS = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_LND = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_PAPF = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_ETC = 0 }
			politics_CGF_agreement_change = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.2.a
		effect_tooltip = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = has_happened_tooltip
			custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_CNS_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_PSF_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = -2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_POU_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = -4 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_CGT_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_PCOP_tt
			set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_seat_change_UA_tt

			custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_CNS = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_agreement_change_CNS_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_PSF = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_agreement_change_PSF_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_PCOP = 2 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_agreement_change_PCOP_tt
			set_temp_variable = { agreement_change_UA = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = politics_CGF_agreement_change_UA_tt
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 10 }
	}
}

			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.11 days = 9 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.12 days = 11 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.13 days = 13 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.14 days = 15 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.15 days = 17 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.16 days = 19 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.17 days = 21 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.18 days = 23 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.19 days = 25 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.20 days = 27 }
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.21 days = 29 }
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.7.a
		add_stability = 0.05

		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_society
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_workers_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_peasants_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_merchants_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_scholars_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_bureaucrats_change = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { pop_satisfaction_army_change = 3 }
		society_pop_satisfaction_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		activate_decision = FCF_politics_election
		set_country_flag = election_time_1936
		unlock_decision_tooltip = FCF_politics_election_1936_CNS_cooperate_with_POU
		unlock_decision_tooltip = FCF_politics_election_1936_CNS_cooperate_with_LND
		unlock_decision_tooltip = FCF_politics_election_1936_PSF_cooperate_with_CGT
		unlock_decision_tooltip = FCF_politics_election_1936_PSF_cooperate_with_URSRS
		unlock_decision_tooltip = FCF_politics_election_1936_CGT_faction_struggle
		custom_effect_tooltip = unlock_in_politics
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
}

	title = fcf_politics_events.22.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.22.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.22.a
		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
		politics_CGF_seat_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_temp_variable = { target_party_id = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_1_change = -6 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_2_change = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_3_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_4_change = 0 }
		politics_party_faction_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.22.b
		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = -4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = -4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = 6 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = 7 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
		politics_CGF_seat_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.22.c
		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = -4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = 20 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = -6 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = -3 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = -4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
		politics_CGF_seat_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.22.d
		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = 14 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = -5 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = -4 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = -2 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
		politics_CGF_seat_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 25 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.23
	title = fcf_politics_events.23.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.23.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.23.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.23.b
		trigger = {
			has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = PSF_cooperate_with_CGT
				has_country_flag = PSF_cooperate_with_URSRS
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution
		set_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_big_win
		FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_big_win_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	after = { hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.26 days = 2 } } }
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.24
	title = fcf_politics_events.24.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.24.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.24.a
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325 }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance_not_325 }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance_not_325_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise }
				clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise
			}
			FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_not_325 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism }
				clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism
			}
			FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_not_325_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 0 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.24.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325 }
			clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325 }
			clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325 }
			clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_not_325 }
			clr_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_not_325
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism }
			FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 100 }
	}
	after = { hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.26 days = 2 } } }
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.25
	title = fcf_politics_events.25.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.25.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.25.a
		set_temp_variable = { CGT_seat_transfer = CGT_faction^2 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { CGT_seat_transfer = 0.01 }
		multiply_temp_variable = { CGT_seat_transfer = seat_CGT }
		round_variable = CGT_seat_transfer
		set_temp_variable = { POU_seat_gain = CGT_seat_transfer }
		set_temp_variable = { CGT_seat_loss = POU_seat_gain }
		multiply_temp_variable = { CGT_seat_loss = -1 }

		custom_effect_tooltip = effect_politics
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CNS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PSF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_POU = POU_seat_gain }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_CGT = CGT_seat_loss }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PCOP = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_UA = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_URSRS = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_LND = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_PAPF = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { seat_change_ETC = 0 }
		politics_CGF_seat_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_temp_variable = { target_party_id = 3 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_1_change = -15 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_2_change = 15 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_3_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_4_change = 0 }
		politics_party_faction_change = yes

		custom_effect_tooltip = tooltip_white_line
		set_temp_variable = { target_party_id = 4 }
		set_temp_variable = { CGT_faction_transfer = CGT_faction^2 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_1_change = CGT_faction_transfer }
		multiply_temp_variable = { CGT_faction_transfer = -1 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_2_change = CGT_faction_transfer }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_3_change = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { faction_4_change = 0 }
		politics_party_faction_change = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
}

country_event = {
	id = fcf_politics_events.26
	title = fcf_politics_events.26.title
	desc = fcf_politics_events.26.desc

	is_triggered_only = yes
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.a
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325
			}
			check_variable = { POU_faction^1 > 50 }
		}
		CSP_appoint_1_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.b
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325
			}
			check_variable = { POU_faction^1 < 50 }
			check_variable = { CGT_faction^2 > 50 }
		}
		CSP_appoint_2_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.32 days = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.c
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_people_front_not_325
			}
			check_variable = { POU_faction^1 < 50 }
			check_variable = { CGT_faction^2 < 50 }
		}
		CSP_appoint_7_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.32 days = 14 } }
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.d
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_worker_peasant_alliance_not_325
			}
		}
		CSP_appoint_10_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.e
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_not_325
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_big_win
			}
		}
		CSP_appoint_4_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.f
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_big_win
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_french_revolution_compromise
			}
			has_country_flag = CGT_in_gov
		}
		CSP_appoint_9_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	option = {
		name = fcf_politics_events.26.g
		trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism
				has_country_flag = FCF_politics_election_1936_pure_syndicalism_not_325
			}
		}
		CSP_appoint_6_effect = yes
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = POU_in_gov }
				country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.32 days = 14 }
			}
		}
		ai_chance = { base = 30 }
	}
	after = { 
		hidden_effect = { 
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.27 days = 2 } 
			country_event = { id = fcf_politics_events.37 days = 14 }
		} 
	}
}
